# 부록 C1. 미드저니 프롬프트 — 영웅 (196 + 레온·엘리아)

자동 생성: `python tools/gen_prompts.py`. 각 영웅 3종(초상 1:1 / 전신 2:3 / 유닛 시트 3:2). `<SREF_*>`는 앵커 세트 확정 후 치환, `<PORTRAIT_URL>`은 확정된 초상 이미지 URL.

## P000 레온 바르크 (자유용병·결투가·T3)

**portrait**

```
character portrait, bust shot, three-quarter view, face fills half the frame, protagonist, wandering mercenary man in his late twenties, grey wolf guild cloak, dented steel breastplate, two swords, determined tired eyes, stubble, swift duelist with paired blades mid-flourish, mercenary aesthetic, mismatched practical gear, grey wolf guild badge, worn leather and dented steel, road-dusted, fine gear with faction insignia and one magical element, color palette #6B7280 primary, #7A5230 secondary, #C9CED6 accent, materials leather, grey-wolf crest, bounty parchment, lighting warm tavern lamplight, simple gradient background in the faction primary color with faint ash particles, painterly digital illustration, stylized dark fantasy, ash-grey atmosphere with ember-orange accents, dramatic rim light, muted palette with one saturated accent, cinematic composition, high-end mobile RPG splash art quality, clean readable silhouette, no text --ar 1:1 --stylize 200 --sref <SREF_PORTRAIT> --no text, watermark, logo, signature, frame, border, blurry, extra limbs, deformed hands
```

